    19→// TestShadow_FullWorkflow tests the complete shadow workflow as described in
    20→// docs/requirements/shadow-strategy/example.md
    21→//
    22→// This test simulates Alice's workflow:
    23→// 1. Start session, create checkpoints
    24→// 2. Rewind to earlier checkpoint
    25→// 3. Create new checkpoint after rewind
    26→// 4. User commits (triggers condensation)
    27→// 5. Continue working after commit (new shadow branch)
    28→// 6. User commits again (second condensation)
    29→// 7. Verify final state
    30→func TestShadow_FullWorkflow(t *testing.T) {
    31→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    32→	defer env.Cleanup()
    33→
    34→	// ========================================
    35→	// Phase 1: Setup
    36→	// ========================================
    37→	env.InitRepo()
    38→
    39→	// Create initial commit on main
    40→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
    41→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    42→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    43→
    44→	// Switch to feature branch (shadow skips main/master)
    45→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/auth")
    46→
    47→	// Initialize Entire AFTER branch switch to avoid go-git cleaning untracked files
    48→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    49→
    50→	initialHead := env.GetHeadHash()
    51→	t.Logf("Initial HEAD on feature/auth: %s", initialHead[:7])
    52→
    53→	// ========================================
    54→	// Phase 2: Session Start & First Checkpoint
    55→	// ========================================
    56→	t.Log("Phase 2: Starting session and creating first checkpoint")
    57→
    58→	session := env.NewSession()
    59→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
    60→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Verify session state file exists in .git/entire-sessions/
    64→	sessionStateDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
    65→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessionStateDir)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session state dir: %v", err)
    68→	}
    69→	if len(entries) == 0 {
    70→		t.Error("Expected session state file in .git/entire-sessions/")
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Create first file (src/auth.go)
    74→	authV1 := "package auth\n\nfunc Authenticate(user, pass string) bool {\n\treturn false\n}"
    75→	env.WriteFile("src/auth.go", authV1)
    76→
    77→	session.CreateTranscript(
    78→		"Create authentication module",
    79→		[]FileChange{{Path: "src/auth.go", Content: authV1}},
    80→	)
    81→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    82→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	// Verify shadow branch created with correct naming (entire/<base-sha[:7]>)
    86→	expectedShadowBranch := "entire/" + initialHead[:7]
    87→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch) {
    88→		t.Errorf("Expected shadow branch %s to exist", expectedShadowBranch)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Verify 1 rewind point
    92→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
    93→	if len(points) != 1 {
    94→		t.Fatalf("Expected 1 rewind point after first checkpoint, got %d", len(points))
    95→	}
    96→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1 created: %s", points[0].Message)
    97→
    98→	// ========================================
    99→	// Phase 3: Second Checkpoint (continuing same session)
   100→	// ========================================
   101→	t.Log("Phase 3: Creating second checkpoint (continuing same session)")
   102→
   103→	// Continue the same session (not a new session) - this is the expected Claude behavior
   104→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   105→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Create hash.go and modify auth.go
   109→	hashV1 := "package auth\n\nimport \"crypto/sha256\"\n\nfunc HashPassword(pass string) []byte {\n\th := sha256.Sum256([]byte(pass))\n\treturn h[:]\n}"
   110→	authV2 := "package auth\n\nfunc Authenticate(user, pass string) bool {\n\t// TODO: use hashed passwords\n\treturn false\n}"
   111→	env.WriteFile("src/hash.go", hashV1)
   112→	env.WriteFile("src/auth.go", authV2)
   113→
   114→	// Reset transcript builder for the new checkpoint
   115→	session.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   116→	session.CreateTranscript(
   117→		"Add password hashing",
   118→		[]FileChange{
   119→			{Path: "src/hash.go", Content: hashV1},
   120→			{Path: "src/auth.go", Content: authV2},
   121→		},
   122→	)
   123→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   124→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Verify 2 rewind points on same shadow branch
   128→	points = env.GetRewindPoints()
   129→	if len(points) != 2 {
   130→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points after second checkpoint, got %d", len(points))
   131→	}
   132→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2 created: %s", points[0].Message)
   133→
   134→	// Verify both files exist
   135→	if !env.FileExists("src/hash.go") {
   136→		t.Error("src/hash.go should exist after checkpoint 2")
   137→	}
   138→	if content := env.ReadFile("src/auth.go"); content != authV2 {
   139→		t.Errorf("src/auth.go should have v2 content, got: %s", content)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// ========================================
   143→	// Phase 4: Rewind to First Checkpoint
   144→	// ========================================
   145→	t.Log("Phase 4: Rewinding to first checkpoint")
   146→
   147→	// Find checkpoint 1 by message (the one for "Create authentication module")
   148→	var checkpoint1ID string
   149→	for _, p := range points {
   150→		if p.Message == "Create authentication module" {
   151→			checkpoint1ID = p.ID
   152→			break
   153→		}
   154→	}
   155→	if checkpoint1ID == "" {
   156→		t.Fatalf("Could not find checkpoint for 'Create authentication module' in %d points", len(points))
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1ID); err != nil {
   160→		t.Fatalf("Rewind to checkpoint 1 failed: %v", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Verify hash.go was removed (it was only added in checkpoint 2)
   164→	if env.FileExists("src/hash.go") {
   165→		t.Error("src/hash.go should NOT exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Verify auth.go restored to v1 (without the "TODO: use hashed passwords" comment)
   169→	content := env.ReadFile("src/auth.go")
   170→	if content != authV1 {
   171→		t.Errorf("src/auth.go should be restored to v1 after rewind, got: %s", content)
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// Verify HEAD unchanged (shadow doesn't modify user's branch)
   175→	if head := env.GetHeadHash(); head != initialHead {
   176→		t.Errorf("HEAD should be unchanged after rewind, got %s, want %s", head[:7], initialHead[:7])
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Verify shadow branch still exists (history preserved)
   180→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch) {
   181→		t.Errorf("Shadow branch %s should still exist after rewind", expectedShadowBranch)
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// ========================================
   185→	// Phase 5: New Checkpoint After Rewind (continue same session)
   186→	// ========================================
   187→	t.Log("Phase 5: Creating checkpoint after rewind (continuing same session)")
   188→
   189→	// Continue the same session after rewind
   190→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (after rewind) failed: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Reset transcript builder for next checkpoint
   195→	session.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   196→
   197→	// Create bcrypt.go instead (different approach)
   198→	bcryptV1 := "package auth\n\nimport \"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt\"\n\nfunc HashPassword(pass string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword([]byte(pass), bcrypt.DefaultCost)\n}"
   199→	authV3 := "package auth\n\nfunc Authenticate(user, pass string) bool {\n\t// Use bcrypt for password hashing\n\treturn false\n}"
   200→	env.WriteFile("src/bcrypt.go", bcryptV1)
   201→	env.WriteFile("src/auth.go", authV3)
   202→
   203→	session.CreateTranscript(
   204→		"Use bcrypt for hashing",
   205→		[]FileChange{
   206→			{Path: "src/bcrypt.go", Content: bcryptV1},
   207→			{Path: "src/auth.go", Content: authV3},
   208→		},
   209→	)
   210→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   211→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 3) failed: %v", err)
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Verify we now have checkpoints (may be 3 if history preserved, or could vary)
   215→	points = env.GetRewindPoints()
   216→	if len(points) < 1 {
   217→		t.Fatalf("Expected at least 1 rewind point after checkpoint 3, got %d", len(points))
   218→	}
   219→	t.Logf("After rewind and new checkpoint: %d rewind points", len(points))
   220→
   221→	// ========================================
   222→	// Phase 6: User Commits (Condensation)
   223→	// ========================================
   224→	t.Log("Phase 6: User commits - triggering condensation")
   225→
   226→	// Stage and commit with shadow hooks
   227→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add user authentication with bcrypt", "src/auth.go", "src/bcrypt.go")
   228→
   229→	// Get the new commit
   230→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   231→	t.Logf("User commit 1: %s", commit1Hash[:7])
   232→
   233→	// Active branch commits should be clean (no Entire-* trailers)
   234→	commitMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
   235→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Session:") {
   236→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Session trailer (clean history), got: %s", commitMsg)
   237→	}
   238→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Condensation:") {
   239→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Condensation trailer (clean history), got: %s", commitMsg)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Get checkpoint ID by walking history - verifies condensation added the trailer
   243→	checkpoint1ID = env.GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory()
   244→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1 ID: %s", checkpoint1ID)
   245→
   246→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists with checkpoint folder
   247→	if !env.BranchExists("entire/sessions") {
   248→		t.Error("entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   249→	}
   250→
   251→	// Verify checkpoint folder contents (check via git show)
   252→	// Uses sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/metadata.json
   253→	checkpointPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint1ID) + "/metadata.json"
   254→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", checkpointPath) {
   255→		t.Errorf("Checkpoint folder should contain metadata.json at %s", checkpointPath)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Clear session state to simulate session completion (avoids concurrent session warning)
   259→	if err := env.ClearSessionState(session.ID); err != nil {
   260→		t.Fatalf("ClearSessionState failed: %v", err)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	// ========================================
   264→	// Phase 7: Continue Working After Commit
   265→	// ========================================
   266→	t.Log("Phase 7: Continuing work after user commit")
   267→
   268→	// Verify HEAD changed
   269→	if commit1Hash == initialHead {
   270→		t.Error("HEAD should have changed after user commit")
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Start new session
   274→	session4 := env.NewSession()
   275→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session4.ID); err != nil {
   276→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (session4) failed: %v", err)
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Create session management file
   280→	sessionMgmt := "package auth\n\nimport \"time\"\n\ntype Session struct {\n\tUserID string\n\tExpiry time.Time\n}"
   281→	env.WriteFile("src/session.go", sessionMgmt)
   282→
   283→	session4.CreateTranscript(
   284→		"Add session management",
   285→		[]FileChange{{Path: "src/session.go", Content: sessionMgmt}},
   286→	)
   287→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session4.ID, session4.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   288→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 4) failed: %v", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Verify NEW shadow branch created (based on new HEAD)
   292→	expectedShadowBranch2 := "entire/" + commit1Hash[:7]
   293→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch2) {
   294→		t.Errorf("Expected new shadow branch %s after commit", expectedShadowBranch2)
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Verify it's different from the first shadow branch
   298→	if expectedShadowBranch == expectedShadowBranch2 {
   299→		t.Error("New shadow branch should have different name than first (different base commit)")
   300→	}
   301→	t.Logf("New shadow branch after commit: %s", expectedShadowBranch2)
   302→
   303→	// ========================================
   304→	// Phase 8: Second User Commit
   305→	// ========================================
   306→	t.Log("Phase 8: Second user commit")
   307→
   308→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add session management", "src/session.go")
   309→
   310→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   311→	t.Logf("User commit 2: %s", commit2Hash[:7])
   312→
   313→	// Verify commit is clean (no trailers)
   314→	commitMsg2 := env.GetCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   315→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg2, "Entire-Session:") {
   316→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Session trailer (clean history), got: %s", commitMsg2)
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Get checkpoint ID from commit message trailer (not from timestamp-based matching
   320→	// which is flaky when commits happen within the same second)
   321→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   322→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2 ID: %s", checkpoint2ID)
   323→
   324→	// Verify DIFFERENT checkpoint ID
   325→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
   326→		t.Errorf("Second commit should have different checkpoint ID: %s vs %s", checkpoint1ID, checkpoint2ID)
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	// Verify second checkpoint folder exists (uses sharded path)
   330→	checkpoint2Path := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint2ID) + "/metadata.json"
   331→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", checkpoint2Path) {
   332→		t.Errorf("Second checkpoint folder should exist at %s", checkpoint2Path)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	// ========================================
   336→	// Phase 9: Verify Final State
   337→	// ========================================
   338→	t.Log("Phase 9: Verifying final state")
   339→
   340→	// 2 user commits on feature branch
   341→	// Both should be clean (no Entire-* trailers)
   342→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Session:") || strings.Contains(commitMsg2, "Entire-Session:") {
   343→		t.Error("Commits should NOT have Entire-Session trailer (clean history)")
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	// 2 checkpoint folders in entire/sessions (Already verified above)
   347→
   348→	// Verify all expected files exist in working directory
   349→	expectedFiles := []string{"README.md", "src/auth.go", "src/bcrypt.go", "src/session.go"}
   350→	for _, f := range expectedFiles {
   351→		if !env.FileExists(f) {
   352→			t.Errorf("Expected file %s to exist in final state", f)
   353→		}
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	// Verify shadow branches exist (can be pruned later)
   357→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch) {
   358→		t.Logf("Note: First shadow branch %s may have been cleaned up", expectedShadowBranch)
   359→	}
   360→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch2) {
   361→		t.Logf("Note: Second shadow branch %s may have been cleaned up", expectedShadowBranch2)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	t.Log("Shadow full workflow test completed successfully!")
   365→}
   366→
   367→// TestShadow_SessionStateLocation verifies session state is stored in .git/
   368→// (not .entire/) so it's never accidentally committed.
   369→func TestShadow_SessionStateLocation(t *testing.T) {
   370→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   371→	defer env.Cleanup()
   372→
   373→	env.InitRepo()
   374→
   375→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   376→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   377→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   378→
   379→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   380→
   381→	// Initialize AFTER branch switch
   382→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   383→
   384→	session := env.NewSession()
   385→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   386→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	// Session state should be in .git/entire-sessions/, NOT .entire/
   390→	gitSessionDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
   391→	entireSessionDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "entire-sessions")
   392→
   393→	if _, err := os.Stat(gitSessionDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   394→		t.Error("Session state directory should exist at .git/entire-sessions/")
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireSessionDir); err == nil {
   398→		t.Error("Session state should NOT be in .entire/entire-sessions/")
   399→	}
   400→}
   401→
   402→// TestShadow_MultipleConcurrentSessions tests that starting a second Claude session
   403→// while another session has uncommitted checkpoints triggers a warning.
   404→// The first prompt is blocked with continue:false, subsequent prompts proceed.
   405→func TestShadow_MultipleConcurrentSessions(t *testing.T) {
   406→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   407→	defer env.Cleanup()
   408→
   409→	env.InitRepo()
   410→
   411→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   412→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   413→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   414→
   415→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   416→
   417→	// Initialize AFTER branch switch
   418→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   419→
   420→	// Start first session
   421→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   422→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   423→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (session1) failed: %v", err)
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	// Create a checkpoint for session1 (this creates the shadow branch)
   427→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   428→	session1.CreateTranscript("Add file", []FileChange{{Path: "file.txt", Content: "content"}})
   429→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   430→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (session1) failed: %v", err)
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	// Verify session state file exists
   434→	sessionStateDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
   435→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessionStateDir)
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session state dir: %v", err)
   438→	}
   439→	if len(entries) != 1 {
   440→		t.Errorf("Expected 1 session state file, got %d", len(entries))
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Starting a second session while session1 has uncommitted checkpoints triggers warning
   444→	// The hook outputs JSON {"continue":false,...} but returns nil (success)
   445→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   446→	err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID)
   447→	// The hook succeeds (returns nil) but outputs JSON with continue:false
   448→	// The test infrastructure treats this as success since the hook didn't return an error
   449→	if err != nil {
   450→		t.Logf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit returned error (expected in some cases): %v", err)
   451→	}
   452→
   453→	// Verify session2 state file was created with ConcurrentWarningShown flag
   454→	// This is set by the hook when it outputs continue:false
   455→	entries, err = os.ReadDir(sessionStateDir)
   456→	if err != nil {
   457→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session state dir: %v", err)
   458→	}
   459→	if len(entries) != 2 {
   460→		t.Errorf("Expected 2 session state files after second session attempt, got %d", len(entries))
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// Clear session1 state file - this makes the shadow branch "orphaned"
   464→	if err := env.ClearSessionState(session1.ID); err != nil {
   465→		t.Fatalf("ClearSessionState failed: %v", err)
   466→	}
   467→
   468→	// Session2 had ConcurrentWarningShown=true, but now the conflict is resolved
   469→	// (session1 state cleared), so the warning flag is cleared and hooks proceed normally.
   470→	// The orphaned shadow branch (from session1) is reset, allowing session2 to proceed.
   471→	err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID)
   472→	if err != nil {
   473→		t.Errorf("Expected success after orphaned shadow branch is reset, got: %v", err)
   474→	} else {
   475→		t.Log("Session2 proceeded after orphaned shadow branch was reset")
   476→	}
   477→}
   478→
   479→// TestShadow_ShadowBranchMigrationOnPull verifies that when the base commit changes
   480→// (e.g., after stash → pull → apply), the shadow branch is moved to the new commit.
   481→func TestShadow_ShadowBranchMigrationOnPull(t *testing.T) {
   482→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   483→	defer env.Cleanup()
   484→
   485→	env.InitRepo()
   486→
   487→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   488→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   489→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   490→
   491→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   492→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   493→
   494→	originalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   495→	originalShadowBranch := "entire/" + originalHead[:7]
   496→
   497→	// Start session and create checkpoint
   498→	session := env.NewSession()
   499→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   500→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   504→	session.CreateTranscript("Add file", []FileChange{{Path: "file.txt", Content: "content"}})
   505→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   506→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Verify shadow branch exists at original commit
   510→	if !env.BranchExists(originalShadowBranch) {
   511→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist", originalShadowBranch)
   512→	}
   513→	t.Logf("Original shadow branch: %s", originalShadowBranch)
   514→
   515→	// Simulate pull: create a new commit (simulating what pull would do)
   516→	// In real scenario: stash → pull → apply
   517→	// Here we just create a commit to simulate HEAD moving
   518→	env.WriteFile("pulled.txt", "from remote")
   519→	env.GitAdd("pulled.txt")
   520→	env.GitCommit("Simulated pull commit")
   521→
   522→	newHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   523→	newShadowBranch := "entire/" + newHead[:7]
   524→	t.Logf("After simulated pull: old=%s new=%s", originalHead[:7], newHead[:7])
   525→
   526→	// Restore the file (simulating stash apply)
   527→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   528→
   529→	// Next prompt should migrate the shadow branch
   530→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   531→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit after pull failed: %v", err)
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	// Verify old shadow branch is gone and new one exists
   535→	if env.BranchExists(originalShadowBranch) {
   536→		t.Errorf("Old shadow branch %s should be deleted after migration", originalShadowBranch)
   537→	}
   538→	if !env.BranchExists(newShadowBranch) {
   539→		t.Errorf("New shadow branch %s should exist after migration", newShadowBranch)
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Verify we can still create checkpoints on the new shadow branch
   543→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "more content")
   544→	session.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   545→	session.CreateTranscript("Add file2", []FileChange{{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "more content"}})
   546→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   547→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop after migration failed: %v", err)
   548→	}
   549→
   550→	// Verify session state has updated base commit and preserves agent type
   551→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   554→	}
   555→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   556→		t.Errorf("Session base commit should be %s, got %s", newHead[:7], state.BaseCommit[:7])
   557→	}
   558→	if state.CheckpointCount != 2 {
   559→		t.Errorf("Expected 2 checkpoints after migration, got %d", state.CheckpointCount)
   560→	}
   561→	// Verify agent_type is preserved across checkpoints and migration
   562→	expectedAgentType := agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
   563→	if state.AgentType != expectedAgentType {
   564→		t.Errorf("Session AgentType should be %q, got %q", expectedAgentType, state.AgentType)
   565→	}
   566→
   567→	t.Log("Shadow branch successfully migrated after base commit change")
   568→}
   569→
   570→// TestShadow_ShadowBranchNaming verifies shadow branches follow the
   571→// entire/<base-sha[:7]> naming convention.
   572→func TestShadow_ShadowBranchNaming(t *testing.T) {
   573→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   574→	defer env.Cleanup()
   575→
   576→	env.InitRepo()
   577→
   578→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   579→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   580→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   581→
   582→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   583→
   584→	// Initialize AFTER branch switch
   585→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   586→
   587→	baseHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   588→
   589→	session := env.NewSession()
   590→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   591→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   595→	session.CreateTranscript("Add file", []FileChange{{Path: "file.txt", Content: "content"}})
   596→
   597→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   598→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	// Verify shadow branch name matches entire/<base-sha[:7]>
   602→	expectedBranch := "entire/" + baseHead[:7]
   603→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedBranch) {
   604→		t.Errorf("Shadow branch should be named %s", expectedBranch)
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	// List all entire/ branches
   608→	branches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   609→	t.Logf("Found entire/ branches: %v", branches)
   610→
   611→	foundExpected := false
   612→	for _, b := range branches {
   613→		if b == expectedBranch {
   614→			foundExpected = true
   615→			break
   616→		}
   617→	}
   618→	if !foundExpected {
   619→		t.Errorf("Expected branch %s not found in %v", expectedBranch, branches)
   620→	}
   621→}
   622→
   623→// TestShadow_TranscriptCondensation verifies that session transcripts are
   624→// included in the entire/sessions branch during condensation.
   625→func TestShadow_TranscriptCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   626→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   627→	defer env.Cleanup()
   628→
   629→	// Setup
   630→	env.InitRepo()
   631→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   632→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   633→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   634→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   635→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   636→
   637→	// Start session and create checkpoint with transcript
   638→	session := env.NewSession()
   639→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   640→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	// Create a file change
   644→	content := "package main\n\nfunc main() {\n\tprintln(\"hello\")\n}\n"
   645→	env.WriteFile("main.go", content)
   646→
   647→	// Create transcript with meaningful content
   648→	session.CreateTranscript(
   649→		"Create main.go with hello world",
   650→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: content}},
   651→	)
   652→
   653→	// Save checkpoint (this stores transcript in shadow branch)
   654→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   655→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   659→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add main.go", "main.go")
   660→
   661→	// Get checkpoint ID from entire/sessions branch (not from commit message)
   662→	checkpointID := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   663→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   664→
   665→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists
   666→	if !env.BranchExists("entire/sessions") {
   667→		t.Fatal("entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   668→	}
   669→
   670→	// Verify metadata.json exists (uses sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/)
   671→	shardedPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpointID)
   672→	metadataPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
   673→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", metadataPath) {
   674→		t.Errorf("metadata.json should exist at %s", metadataPath)
   675→	}
   676→
   677→	// Verify transcript file exists
   678→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
   679→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", transcriptPath) {
   680→		t.Errorf("Transcript (%s) should exist at %s", paths.TranscriptFileName, transcriptPath)
   681→	} else {
   682→		t.Log("✓ Transcript file exists in checkpoint")
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Verify content_hash.txt exists (computed from transcript)
   686→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/content_hash.txt"
   687→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", hashPath) {
   688→		t.Errorf("content_hash.txt should exist at %s", hashPath)
   689→	}
   690→
   691→	// Verify metadata.json can be read and parsed
   692→	metadataContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", metadataPath)
   693→	if !found {
   694→		t.Fatal("metadata.json should be readable")
   695→	}
   696→	var metadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   697→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(metadataContent), &metadata); err != nil {
   698→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse metadata.json: %v", err)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	// Verify agent field is populated (from ClaudeCodeAgent.Type())
   702→	expectedAgent := agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
   703→	if metadata.Agent != expectedAgent {
   704→		t.Errorf("metadata.json Agent = %q, want %q", metadata.Agent, expectedAgent)
   705→	} else {
   706→		t.Logf("✓ metadata.json has agent: %q", metadata.Agent)
   707→	}
   708→}
   709→
   710→// TestShadow_FullTranscriptContext verifies that each checkpoint includes
   711→// the full session transcript, preserving complete history across commits.
   712→//
   713→// This tests transcript preservation:
   714→// - First commit: context.md includes prompts 1-2
   715→// - Second commit: context.md includes prompts 1-3 (full transcript preserved)
   716→func TestShadow_FullTranscriptContext(t *testing.T) {
   717→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   718→	defer env.Cleanup()
   719→
   720→	// Setup repository
   721→	env.InitRepo()
   722→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   723→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   724→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   725→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/incremental")
   726→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   727→
   728→	t.Log("Phase 1: First session with two prompts")
   729→
   730→	// Start first session
   731→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   732→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   733→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   734→	}
   735→
   736→	// First prompt: create file A
   737→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
   738→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
   739→
   740→	// Build transcript with first prompt
   741→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A in a.go")
   742→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
   743→	toolID1 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
   744→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
   745→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
   746→
   747→	// Second prompt in same session: create file B
   748→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
   749→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
   750→
   751→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Now create function B in b.go")
   752→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function B for you.")
   753→	toolID2 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
   754→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
   755→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function B!")
   756→
   757→	// Write transcript
   758→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   759→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   760→	}
   761→
   762→	// Save checkpoint (triggers SaveChanges)
   763→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   764→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   765→	}
   766→
   767→	t.Log("Phase 2: First user commit")
   768→
   769→	// User commits
   770→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add functions A and B", "a.go", "b.go")
   771→
   772→	// Get first checkpoint ID from commit message trailer
   773→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   774→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
   775→	t.Logf("First checkpoint ID: %s", checkpoint1ID)
   776→
   777→	// Verify first checkpoint has both prompts (uses sharded path)
   778→	shardedPath1 := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint1ID)
   779→	promptPath1 := shardedPath1 + "/prompt.txt"
   780→	prompt1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", promptPath1)
   781→	if !found {
   782→		t.Errorf("prompt.txt should exist at %s", promptPath1)
   783→	} else {
   784→		t.Logf("First prompt.txt content:\n%s", prompt1Content)
   785→		// Should contain both "Create function A" and "create function B"
   786→		if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "Create function A") {
   787→			t.Error("First prompt.txt should contain 'Create function A'")
   788→		}
   789→		if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "create function B") {
   790→			t.Error("First prompt.txt should contain 'create function B'")
   791→		}
   792→	}
   793→
   794→	contextPath1 := shardedPath1 + "/context.md"
   795→	context1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", contextPath1)
   796→	if !found {
   797→		t.Errorf("context.md should exist at %s", contextPath1)
   798→	} else {
   799→		t.Logf("First context.md content:\n%s", context1Content)
   800→	}
   801→
   802→	t.Log("Phase 3: Continue session with third prompt")
   803→
   804→	// Continue the session with a new prompt
   805→	// First, simulate another user prompt submit to track the new base
   806→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   807→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (continued) failed: %v", err)
   808→	}
   809→
   810→	// Third prompt: create file C
   811→	fileCContent := "package main\n\nfunc C() {}\n"
   812→	env.WriteFile("c.go", fileCContent)
   813→
   814→	// Add to transcript (continuing from previous)
   815→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Finally, create function C in c.go")
   816→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function C for you.")
   817→	toolID3 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "c.go", fileCContent)
   818→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID3)
   819→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function C!")
   820→
   821→	// Write updated transcript
   822→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   823→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write updated transcript: %v", err)
   824→	}
   825→
   826→	// Save checkpoint
   827→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   828→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (second) failed: %v", err)
   829→	}
   830→
   831→	t.Log("Phase 4: Second user commit")
   832→
   833→	// User commits again
   834→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function C", "c.go")
   835→
   836→	// Get second checkpoint ID from commit message trailer
   837→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   838→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   839→	t.Logf("Second checkpoint ID: %s", checkpoint2ID)
   840→
   841→	// Verify different checkpoint IDs
   842→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
   843→		t.Errorf("Second commit should have different checkpoint ID: %s vs %s", checkpoint1ID, checkpoint2ID)
   844→	}
   845→
   846→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify full transcript preserved in second checkpoint")
   847→
   848→	// Verify second checkpoint has the FULL transcript (all three prompts)
   849→	shardedPath2 := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint2ID)
   850→	promptPath2 := shardedPath2 + "/prompt.txt"
   851→	prompt2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", promptPath2)
   852→	if !found {
   853→		t.Errorf("prompt.txt should exist at %s", promptPath2)
   854→	} else {
   855→		t.Logf("Second prompt.txt content:\n%s", prompt2Content)
   856→
   857→		// Should contain all prompts (full transcript preserved)
   858→		if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "create function C") {
   859→			t.Error("Second prompt.txt should contain 'create function C'")
   860→		}
   861→		if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "Create function A") {
   862→			t.Error("Second prompt.txt should contain 'Create function A' (full transcript)")
   863→		}
   864→		if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "create function B") {
   865→			t.Error("Second prompt.txt should contain 'create function B' (full transcript)")
   866→		}
   867→	}
   868→
   869→	contextPath2 := shardedPath2 + "/context.md"
   870→	context2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", contextPath2)
   871→	if !found {
   872→		t.Errorf("context.md should exist at %s", contextPath2)
   873→	} else {
   874→		t.Logf("Second context.md content:\n%s", context2Content)
   875→
   876→		// Should contain full transcript context
   877→		if !strings.Contains(context2Content, "Create function A") {
   878→			t.Error("Second context.md should contain 'Create function A' (full transcript)")
   879→		}
   880→	}
   881→
   882→	t.Log("Shadow full transcript context test completed successfully!")
   883→}
   884→
   885→// TestShadow_RewindAndCondensation verifies that after rewinding to an earlier
   886→// checkpoint, the checkpoint only includes prompts up to that point.
   887→//
   888→// Workflow:
   889→// 1. Create checkpoint 1 (prompt 1)
   890→// 2. Create checkpoint 2 (prompt 2)
   891→// 3. Rewind to checkpoint 1
   892→// 4. User commits
   893→// 5. Verify checkpoint only contains prompt 1 (NOT prompt 2)
   894→func TestShadow_RewindAndCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   895→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   896→	defer env.Cleanup()
   897→
   898→	// Setup repository
   899→	env.InitRepo()
   900→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   901→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   902→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   903→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/rewind-test")
   904→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   905→
   906→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create first checkpoint with prompt 1")
   907→
   908→	session := env.NewSession()
   909→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   910→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   911→	}
   912→
   913→	// First prompt: create file A
   914→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
   915→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
   916→
   917→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A in a.go")
   918→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
   919→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
   920→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
   921→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
   922→
   923→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   924→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   928→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
   929→	}
   930→
   931→	// Get checkpoint 1 for later
   932→	rewindPoints := env.GetRewindPoints()
   933→	if len(rewindPoints) != 1 {
   934→		t.Fatalf("Expected 1 rewind point after checkpoint 1, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
   935→	}
   936→	checkpoint1 := rewindPoints[0]
   937→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1: %s - %s", checkpoint1.ID[:7], checkpoint1.Message)
   938→
   939→	t.Log("Phase 2: Create second checkpoint with prompt 2")
   940→
   941→	// Second prompt: modify file A (a different approach)
   942→	fileAModified := "package main\n\nfunc A() {\n\t// Modified version\n}\n"
   943→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAModified)
   944→
   945→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Actually, modify function A to have a comment")
   946→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll modify function A for you.")
   947→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAModified)
   948→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
   949→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done modifying function A!")
   950→
   951→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   952→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   953→	}
   954→
   955→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   956→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
   957→	}
   958→
   959→	rewindPoints = env.GetRewindPoints()
   960→	if len(rewindPoints) != 2 {
   961→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points after checkpoint 2, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
   962→	}
   963→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2: %s - %s", rewindPoints[0].ID[:7], rewindPoints[0].Message)
   964→
   965→	// Verify file has modified content
   966→	currentContent := env.ReadFile("a.go")
   967→	if currentContent != fileAModified {
   968→		t.Errorf("a.go should have modified content before rewind")
   969→	}
   970→
   971→	t.Log("Phase 3: Rewind to checkpoint 1")
   972→
   973→	// Rewind using the CLI (which calls the strategy internally)
   974→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1.ID); err != nil {
   975→		t.Fatalf("Rewind failed: %v", err)
   976→	}
   977→
   978→	// Verify file content is restored to checkpoint 1
   979→	restoredContent := env.ReadFile("a.go")
   980→	if restoredContent != fileAContent {
   981→		t.Errorf("a.go should have original content after rewind.\nExpected:\n%s\nGot:\n%s", fileAContent, restoredContent)
   982→	}
   983→	t.Log("Files successfully restored to checkpoint 1")
   984→
   985→	t.Log("Phase 4: User commits after rewind")
   986→
   987→	// User commits - this should trigger condensation
   988→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function A (reverted)", "a.go")
   989→
   990→	// Get checkpoint ID from commit message trailer
   991→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   992→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   993→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   994→
   995→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify checkpoint only contains prompt 1")
   996→
   997→	// Check prompt.txt (uses sharded path)
   998→	shardedPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpointID)
   999→	promptPath := shardedPath + "/prompt.txt"
  1000→	promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", promptPath)
  1001→	if !found {
  1002→		t.Errorf("prompt.txt should exist at %s", promptPath)
  1003→	} else {
  1004→		t.Logf("prompt.txt content:\n%s", promptContent)
  1005→
  1006→		// Should contain prompt 1
  1007→		if !strings.Contains(promptContent, "Create function A") {
  1008→			t.Error("prompt.txt should contain 'Create function A' from checkpoint 1")
  1009→		}
  1010→
  1011→		// Should NOT contain prompt 2 (because we rewound past it)
  1012→		if strings.Contains(promptContent, "modify function A") {
  1013→			t.Error("prompt.txt should NOT contain 'modify function A' - we rewound past that checkpoint")
  1014→		}
  1015→	}
  1016→
  1017→	// Check context.md
  1018→	contextPath := shardedPath + "/context.md"
  1019→	contextContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", contextPath)
  1020→	if !found {
  1021→		t.Errorf("context.md should exist at %s", contextPath)
  1022→	} else {
  1023→		t.Logf("context.md content:\n%s", contextContent)
  1024→
  1025→		// Should NOT contain context from checkpoint 2
  1026→		if strings.Contains(contextContent, "modify function A") {
  1027→			t.Error("context.md should NOT contain 'modify function A' - we rewound past that checkpoint")
  1028→		}
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	t.Log("Shadow rewind and condensation test completed successfully!")
  1032→}
  1033→
  1034→// TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesFromSessionStart tests that files that existed
  1035→// in the working directory (but weren't tracked in git) before the session started are
  1036→// preserved when rewinding. This was a bug where such files were incorrectly deleted.
  1037→func TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesFromSessionStart(t *testing.T) {
  1038→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1039→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1040→
  1041→	// Setup repository with initial commit
  1042→	env.InitRepo()
  1043→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1044→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1045→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1046→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/untracked-test")
  1047→
  1048→	// Create an untracked file BEFORE initializing Entire session
  1049→	// This simulates files like .claude/settings.json created by "entire setup"
  1050→	untrackedContent := `{"key": "value"}`
  1051→	env.WriteFile(".claude/settings.json", untrackedContent)
  1052→
  1053→	// Initialize Entire with manual-commit strategy
  1054→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1055→
  1056→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create first checkpoint")
  1057→
  1058→	session := env.NewSession()
  1059→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1060→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1061→	}
  1062→
  1063→	// First prompt: create file A
  1064→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1065→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1066→
  1067→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A")
  1068→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1069→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1070→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
  1071→
  1072→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1073→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1074→	}
  1075→
  1076→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1077→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
  1078→	}
  1079→
  1080→	rewindPoints := env.GetRewindPoints()
  1081→	if len(rewindPoints) != 1 {
  1082→		t.Fatalf("Expected 1 rewind point, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
  1083→	}
  1084→	checkpoint1 := rewindPoints[0]
  1085→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1: %s", checkpoint1.ID[:7])
  1086→
  1087→	t.Log("Phase 2: Create second checkpoint")
  1088→
  1089→	// Second prompt: create file B
  1090→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1091→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1092→
  1093→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B")
  1094→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1095→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1096→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1097→
  1098→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1099→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1100→	}
  1101→
  1102→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1103→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
  1104→	}
  1105→
  1106→	rewindPoints = env.GetRewindPoints()
  1107→	if len(rewindPoints) != 2 {
  1108→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
  1109→	}
  1110→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2: %s", rewindPoints[0].ID[:7])
  1111→
  1112→	// Verify the untracked file still exists before rewind
  1113→	if !env.FileExists(".claude/settings.json") {
  1114→		t.Fatal("Untracked file .claude/settings.json should exist before rewind")
  1115→	}
  1116→
  1117→	t.Log("Phase 3: Rewind to checkpoint 1")
  1118→
  1119→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1.ID); err != nil {
  1120→		t.Fatalf("Rewind failed: %v", err)
  1121→	}
  1122→
  1123→	// Verify that the untracked file that existed before session start is PRESERVED
  1124→	if !env.FileExists(".claude/settings.json") {
  1125→		t.Error("CRITICAL: .claude/settings.json was deleted during rewind but it existed before the session started!")
  1126→	} else {
  1127→		restoredContent := env.ReadFile(".claude/settings.json")
  1128→		if restoredContent != untrackedContent {
  1129→			t.Errorf("Untracked file content changed.\nExpected:\n%s\nGot:\n%s", untrackedContent, restoredContent)
  1130→		} else {
  1131→			t.Log("✓ Untracked file .claude/settings.json was preserved correctly")
  1132→		}
  1133→	}
  1134→
  1135→	// Verify b.go was deleted (it was created after checkpoint 1)
  1136→	if env.FileExists("b.go") {
  1137→		t.Error("b.go should have been deleted during rewind (it was created after checkpoint 1)")
  1138→	} else {
  1139→		t.Log("✓ b.go was correctly deleted during rewind")
  1140→	}
  1141→
  1142→	// Verify a.go was restored
  1143→	if !env.FileExists("a.go") {
  1144→		t.Error("a.go should exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
  1145→	} else {
  1146→		restoredA := env.ReadFile("a.go")
  1147→		if restoredA != fileAContent {
  1148→			t.Errorf("a.go content incorrect after rewind")
  1149→		} else {
  1150→			t.Log("✓ a.go was correctly restored")
  1151→		}
  1152→	}
  1153→
  1154→	t.Log("Test completed successfully!")
  1155→}
  1156→
  1157→// TestShadow_IntermediateCommitsWithoutPrompts tests that commits without new Claude
  1158→// content do NOT get checkpoint trailers.
  1159→//
  1160→// Scenario:
  1161→// 1. Session starts, work happens, checkpoint created
  1162→// 2. First commit gets a trailer (has new content)
  1163→// 3. User commits unrelated files without new Claude work - NO trailer (no new content)
  1164→// 4. User enters new prompt, creates more files
  1165→// 5. Second commit with Claude content gets a trailer
  1166→func TestShadow_IntermediateCommitsWithoutPrompts(t *testing.T) {
  1167→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1168→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1169→
  1170→	// Setup repository
  1171→	env.InitRepo()
  1172→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1173→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1174→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1175→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/intermediate-commits")
  1176→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1177→
  1178→	t.Log("Phase 1: Start session and create checkpoint")
  1179→
  1180→	session := env.NewSession()
  1181→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1182→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1183→	}
  1184→
  1185→	// First prompt: create file A
  1186→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1187→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1188→
  1189→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A in a.go")
  1190→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
  1191→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1192→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
  1193→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
  1194→
  1195→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1196→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1197→	}
  1198→
  1199→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1200→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1201→	}
  1202→
  1203→	t.Log("Phase 2: First commit (with session content)")
  1204→
  1205→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function A", "a.go")
  1206→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1207→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
  1208→	t.Logf("First commit: %s, checkpoint from trailer: %s", commit1Hash[:7], checkpoint1ID)
  1209→	t.Logf("First commit message:\n%s", env.GetCommitMessage(commit1Hash))
  1210→
  1211→	if checkpoint1ID == "" {
  1212→		t.Fatal("First commit should have a checkpoint ID in its trailer (has new content)")
  1213→	}
  1214→
  1215→	t.Log("Phase 3: Create unrelated file and commit WITHOUT new prompt")
  1216→
  1217→	// User creates an unrelated file and commits without entering a new Claude prompt
  1218→	// Since there's no new session content, this commit should NOT get a trailer
  1219→	env.WriteFile("unrelated.txt", "This is an unrelated file")
  1220→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add unrelated file", "unrelated.txt")
  1221→
  1222→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1223→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
  1224→	t.Logf("Second commit: %s, checkpoint from trailer: %s", commit2Hash[:7], checkpoint2ID)
  1225→	t.Logf("Second commit message:\n%s", env.GetCommitMessage(commit2Hash))
  1226→
  1227→	// Second commit should NOT get a checkpoint ID (no new session content)
  1228→	if checkpoint2ID != "" {
  1229→		t.Errorf("Second commit should NOT have a checkpoint trailer (no new content), got: %s", checkpoint2ID)
  1230→	}
  1231→
  1232→	t.Log("Phase 4: New Claude work and commit")
  1233→
  1234→	// Now user enters new prompt and does more work
  1235→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1236→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1237→	}
  1238→
  1239→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1240→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1241→
  1242→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B in b.go")
  1243→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function B for you.")
  1244→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1245→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1246→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function B!")
  1247→
  1248→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1249→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1250→	}
  1251→
  1252→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1253→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1254→	}
  1255→
  1256→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function B", "b.go")
  1257→
  1258→	commit3Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1259→	checkpoint3ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit3Hash)
  1260→	t.Logf("Third commit: %s, checkpoint from trailer: %s", commit3Hash[:7], checkpoint3ID)
  1261→	t.Logf("Third commit message:\n%s", env.GetCommitMessage(commit3Hash))
  1262→
  1263→	if checkpoint3ID == "" {
  1264→		t.Fatal("Third commit should have a checkpoint ID (has new content)")
  1265→	}
  1266→
  1267→	// First and third checkpoint IDs should be different
  1268→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint3ID {
  1269→		t.Errorf("First and third commits should have different checkpoint IDs: %s vs %s",
  1270→			checkpoint1ID, checkpoint3ID)
  1271→	}
  1272→
  1273→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify checkpoints exist in entire/sessions")
  1274→
  1275→	for _, cpID := range []string{checkpoint1ID, checkpoint3ID} {
  1276→		shardedPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(cpID)
  1277→		metadataPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1278→		if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", metadataPath) {
  1279→			t.Errorf("Checkpoint %s should have metadata.json at %s", cpID, metadataPath)
  1280→		}
  1281→	}
  1282→
  1283→	t.Log("Intermediate commits test completed successfully!")
  1284→}
  1285→
  1286→// TestShadow_FullTranscriptCondensationWithIntermediateCommits tests that full transcripts
  1287→// are preserved across multiple commits.
  1288→//
  1289→// Scenario:
  1290→// 1. Session with prompts A and B, commit 1
  1291→// 2. Continue session with prompt C, commit 2 (without intermediate prompt submit)
  1292→// 3. Verify commit 2's checkpoint has full transcript (A, B, and C)
  1293→func TestShadow_FullTranscriptCondensationWithIntermediateCommits(t *testing.T) {
  1294→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1295→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1296→
  1297→	// Setup repository
  1298→	env.InitRepo()
  1299→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1300→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1301→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1302→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/incremental-intermediate")
  1303→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1304→
  1305→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session with two prompts")
  1306→
  1307→	session := env.NewSession()
  1308→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1309→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1310→	}
  1311→
  1312→	// First prompt
  1313→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1314→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1315→
  1316→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A")
  1317→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1318→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1319→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
  1320→
  1321→	// Second prompt in same session
  1322→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1323→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1324→
  1325→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B")
  1326→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1327→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1328→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1329→
  1330→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1331→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1332→	}
  1333→
  1334→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1335→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1336→	}
  1337→
  1338→	t.Log("Phase 2: First commit")
  1339→
  1340→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add functions A and B", "a.go", "b.go")
  1341→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1342→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
  1343→	t.Logf("First commit: %s, checkpoint: %s", commit1Hash[:7], checkpoint1ID)
  1344→
  1345→	// Verify first checkpoint has prompts A and B
  1346→	shardedPath1 := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint1ID)
  1347→	prompt1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", shardedPath1+"/prompt.txt")
  1348→	if !found {
  1349→		t.Fatal("First checkpoint should have prompt.txt")
  1350→	}
  1351→	if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "function A") || !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "function B") {
  1352→		t.Errorf("First checkpoint should contain prompts A and B, got: %s", prompt1Content)
  1353→	}
  1354→	t.Logf("First checkpoint prompts:\n%s", prompt1Content)
  1355→
  1356→	t.Log("Phase 3: Continue session with third prompt (no SimulateUserPromptSubmit)")
  1357→
  1358→	// Continue working WITHOUT calling SimulateUserPromptSubmit
  1359→	// This simulates the case where HEAD moved but InitializeSession wasn't called
  1360→	fileCContent := "package main\n\nfunc C() {}\n"
  1361→	env.WriteFile("c.go", fileCContent)
  1362→
  1363→	// Add to transcript
  1364→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function C")
  1365→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1366→	toolID3 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "c.go", fileCContent)
  1367→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID3)
  1368→
  1369→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1370→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write updated transcript: %v", err)
  1371→	}
  1372→
  1373→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1374→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (second) failed: %v", err)
  1375→	}
  1376→
  1377→	t.Log("Phase 4: Second commit (without intermediate prompt submit)")
  1378→
  1379→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function C", "c.go")
  1380→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1381→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
  1382→	t.Logf("Second commit: %s, checkpoint: %s", commit2Hash[:7], checkpoint2ID)
  1383→
  1384→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
  1385→		t.Errorf("Commits should have different checkpoint IDs")
  1386→	}
  1387→
  1388→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify second checkpoint has full transcript (A, B, and C)")
  1389→
  1390→	shardedPath2 := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint2ID)
  1391→	prompt2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch("entire/sessions", shardedPath2+"/prompt.txt")
  1392→	if !found {
  1393→		t.Fatal("Second checkpoint should have prompt.txt")
  1394→	}
  1395→
  1396→	t.Logf("Second checkpoint prompts:\n%s", prompt2Content)
  1397→
  1398→	// Should contain all prompts (full transcript preserved)
  1399→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function C") {
  1400→		t.Error("Second checkpoint should contain 'function C'")
  1401→	}
  1402→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function A") {
  1403→		t.Error("Second checkpoint should contain 'function A' (full transcript)")
  1404→	}
  1405→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function B") {
  1406→		t.Error("Second checkpoint should contain 'function B' (full transcript)")
  1407→	}
  1408→
  1409→	t.Log("Full transcript condensation with intermediate commits test completed successfully!")
  1410→}
  1411→
  1412→// TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesWithExistingShadowBranch tests that untracked files
  1413→// present at session start are preserved during rewind, even when the shadow branch already
  1414→// exists from a previous session.
  1415→func TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesWithExistingShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
  1416→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1417→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1418→
  1419→	// Setup repository with initial commit
  1420→	env.InitRepo()
  1421→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1422→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1423→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1424→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/existing-shadow-test")
  1425→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1426→
  1427→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create untracked file before session starts")
  1428→
  1429→	// Create an untracked file BEFORE the first checkpoint
  1430→	// This simulates configuration files that exist before Claude starts
  1431→	untrackedContent := `{"new": "config"}`
  1432→	env.WriteFile(".claude/settings.json", untrackedContent)
  1433→
  1434→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create a previous session to establish shadow branch")
  1435→
  1436→	// First session - creates the shadow branch
  1437→	session1 := env.NewSession()
  1438→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
  1439→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1440→	}
  1441→
  1442→	env.WriteFile("old.go", "package main\n")
  1443→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create old.go")
  1444→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1445→	toolID := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "old.go", "package main\n")
  1446→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID)
  1447→
  1448→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1449→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1450→	}
  1451→
  1452→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1453→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (session 1) failed: %v", err)
  1454→	}
  1455→
  1456→	// Verify shadow branch exists
  1457→	shadowBranchName := "entire/" + env.GetHeadHash()[:7]
  1458→	if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranchName) {
  1459→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist after first session", shadowBranchName)
  1460→	}
  1461→	t.Logf("Shadow branch %s exists from first session", shadowBranchName)
  1462→
  1463→	t.Log("Phase 2: Continue session and create second checkpoint")
  1464→
  1465→	// Continue the SAME session (Claude resumes with the same session ID)
  1466→	// This is the expected behavior - continuing work on the same base commit
  1467→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
  1468→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (continue session) failed: %v", err)
  1469→	}
  1470→
  1471→	// Reset transcript builder for next checkpoint
  1472→	session1.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
  1473→
  1474→	// Second checkpoint of session - should capture .claude/settings.json
  1475→	env.WriteFile("a.go", "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n")
  1476→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create A")
  1477→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1478→	toolID2 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n")
  1479→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1480→
  1481→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1482→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1483→	}
  1484→
  1485→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1486→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
  1487→	}
  1488→
  1489→	rewindPoints := env.GetRewindPoints()
  1490→	if len(rewindPoints) < 2 {
  1491→		t.Fatalf("Expected at least 2 rewind points, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
  1492→	}
  1493→	// Find the most recent checkpoint (checkpoint 2)
  1494→	checkpoint1 := &rewindPoints[0] // Most recent first
  1495→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2: %s", checkpoint1.ID[:7])
  1496→
  1497→	t.Log("Phase 3: Create third checkpoint")
  1498→
  1499→	// Continue the session for the third checkpoint
  1500→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
  1501→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (checkpoint 3) failed: %v", err)
  1502→	}
  1503→
  1504→	// Reset transcript builder for next checkpoint
  1505→	session1.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
  1506→
  1507→	env.WriteFile("b.go", "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n")
  1508→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create B")
  1509→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1510→	toolID3 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n")
  1511→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID3)
  1512→
  1513→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1514→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1515→	}
  1516→
  1517→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1518→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 3) failed: %v", err)
  1519→	}
  1520→
  1521→	t.Log("Phase 4: Rewind to checkpoint 2")
  1522→
  1523→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1.ID); err != nil {
  1524→		t.Fatalf("Rewind failed: %v", err)
  1525→	}
  1526→
  1527→	// Verify that the untracked file that existed at session start is PRESERVED
  1528→	// Since .claude/settings.json was created before checkpoint 1, it's in checkpoint 1's tree
  1529→	// and will flow through to checkpoint 2, so it should be preserved on rewind
  1530→	if !env.FileExists(".claude/settings.json") {
  1531→		t.Error(".claude/settings.json should have been preserved during rewind")
  1532→	} else {
  1533→		restoredContent := env.ReadFile(".claude/settings.json")
  1534→		if restoredContent != untrackedContent {
  1535→			t.Errorf("Untracked file content changed.\nExpected:\n%s\nGot:\n%s", untrackedContent, restoredContent)
  1536→		} else {
  1537→			t.Log("✓ .claude/settings.json was preserved correctly")
  1538→		}
  1539→	}
  1540→
  1541→	// Verify b.go was deleted
  1542→	if env.FileExists("b.go") {
  1543→		t.Error("b.go should have been deleted during rewind")
  1544→	} else {
  1545→		t.Log("✓ b.go was correctly deleted during rewind")
  1546→	}
  1547→
  1548→	t.Log("Test completed successfully!")
  1549→}
  1550→
  1551→// TestShadow_TrailerRemovalSkipsCondensation tests that removing the Entire-Checkpoint
  1552→// trailer during commit message editing causes condensation to be skipped.
  1553→// This allows users to opt-out of linking a commit to their Claude session.
  1554→func TestShadow_TrailerRemovalSkipsCondensation(t *testing.T) {
  1555→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1556→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1557→
  1558→	// Setup
  1559→	env.InitRepo()
  1560→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1561→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1562→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1563→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/trailer-opt-out")
  1564→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1565→
  1566→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create session with content")
  1567→
  1568→	session := env.NewSession()
  1569→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1570→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1571→	}
  1572→
  1573→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1574→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1575→
  1576→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A")
  1577→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
  1578→	toolID := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1579→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID)
  1580→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1581→
  1582→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1583→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1584→	}
  1585→
  1586→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1587→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1588→	}
  1589→
  1590→	t.Log("Phase 2: Commit WITH trailer removed (user opts out)")
  1591→
  1592→	// Use the special helper that removes the trailer before committing
  1593→	env.GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved("Add function A (manual commit)", "a.go")
  1594→
  1595→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1596→	t.Logf("Commit: %s", commitHash[:7])
  1597→
  1598→	// Verify commit does NOT have trailer
  1599→	commitMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(commitHash)
  1600→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
  1601→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Checkpoint trailer (it was removed), got:\n%s", commitMsg)
  1602→	}
  1603→	t.Logf("Commit message (trailer removed):\n%s", commitMsg)
  1604→
  1605→	t.Log("Phase 3: Verify no condensation happened")
  1606→
  1607→	// entire/sessions branch exists (created at setup), but should not have any checkpoint commits yet
  1608→	// since the user removed the trailer
  1609→	latestCheckpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
  1610→	if latestCheckpointID == "" {
  1611→		t.Log("✓ No checkpoint found on entire/sessions branch (no condensation)")
  1612→	} else {
  1613→		// If there is a checkpoint, this is unexpected for this test
  1614→		t.Logf("Found checkpoint ID: %s (should be from previous activity, not this commit)", latestCheckpointID)
  1615→	}
  1616→
  1617→	t.Log("Phase 4: Now commit WITH trailer (user keeps it)")
  1618→
  1619→	// Continue session with new content
  1620→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1621→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1622→	}
  1623→
  1624→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1625→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1626→
  1627→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B")
  1628→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1629→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1630→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1631→
  1632→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1633→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1634→	}
  1635→
  1636→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1637→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1638→	}
  1639→
  1640→	// This time, keep the trailer (normal commit with hooks)
  1641→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function B", "b.go")
  1642→
  1643→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1644→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
  1645→	t.Logf("Second commit: %s, checkpoint: %s", commit2Hash[:7], checkpointID)
  1646→
  1647→	// Verify second commit HAS trailer
  1648→	commit2Msg := env.GetCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
  1649→	if !strings.Contains(commit2Msg, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
  1650→		t.Errorf("Second commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer, got:\n%s", commit2Msg)
  1651→	}
  1652→
  1653→	// Verify condensation happened for second commit
  1654→	if !env.BranchExists("entire/sessions") {
  1655→		t.Fatal("entire/sessions branch should exist after second commit with trailer")
  1656→	}
  1657→
  1658→	// Verify checkpoint exists
  1659→	shardedPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpointID)
  1660→	metadataPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1661→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch("entire/sessions", metadataPath) {
  1662→		t.Errorf("Checkpoint should exist at %s", metadataPath)
  1663→	} else {
  1664→		t.Log("✓ Condensation happened for commit with trailer")
  1665→	}
  1666→
  1667→	t.Log("Trailer removal opt-out test completed successfully!")
  1668→}
  1669→
  1670→// TestShadow_SessionsBranchCommitTrailers verifies that commits on the entire/sessions
  1671→// branch contain the expected trailers: Entire-Session, Entire-Strategy, and Entire-Agent.
  1672→func TestShadow_SessionsBranchCommitTrailers(t *testing.T) {
  1673→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1674→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1675→
  1676→	// Setup
  1677→	env.InitRepo()
  1678→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1679→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1680→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1681→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/trailer-test")
  1682→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1683→
  1684→	// Start session and create checkpoint
  1685→	session := env.NewSession()
  1686→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1687→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1688→	}
  1689→
  1690→	fileContent := "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n"
  1691→	env.WriteFile("main.go", fileContent)
  1692→	session.CreateTranscript("Create main.go", []FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: fileContent}})
  1693→
  1694→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1695→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1696→	}
  1697→
  1698→	// Commit to trigger condensation
  1699→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add main.go", "main.go")
  1700→
  1701→	// Get the commit message on entire/sessions branch
  1702→	sessionsCommitMsg := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch("entire/sessions")
  1703→	t.Logf("entire/sessions commit message:\n%s", sessionsCommitMsg)
  1704→
  1705→	// Verify required trailers are present
  1706→	requiredTrailers := map[string]string{
  1707→		trailers.SessionTrailerKey:  "",                                // Entire-Session: <session-id>
  1708→		trailers.StrategyTrailerKey: strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, // Entire-Strategy: manual-commit
  1709→		trailers.AgentTrailerKey:    "Claude Code",                     // Entire-Agent: Claude Code
  1710→	}
  1711→
  1712→	for trailerKey, expectedValue := range requiredTrailers {
  1713→		if !strings.Contains(sessionsCommitMsg, trailerKey+":") {
  1714→			t.Errorf("entire/sessions commit should have %s trailer", trailerKey)
  1715→			continue
  1716→		}
  1717→
  1718→		// If we have an expected value, verify it
  1719→		if expectedValue != "" {
  1720→			expectedTrailer := trailerKey + ": " + expectedValue
  1721→			if !strings.Contains(sessionsCommitMsg, expectedTrailer) {
  1722→				t.Errorf("entire/sessions commit should have %q, got message:\n%s", expectedTrailer, sessionsCommitMsg)
  1723→			} else {
  1724→				t.Logf("✓ Found trailer: %s", expectedTrailer)
  1725→			}
  1726→		} else {
  1727→			t.Logf("✓ Found trailer: %s", trailerKey)
  1728→		}
  1729→	}
  1730→
  1731→	t.Log("Sessions branch commit trailers test completed successfully!")
  1732→}
  1733→

    13→// TestMain builds the CLI binary once before running all tests.
    14→func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
    15→	// Build binary once to a temp directory
    16→	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "entire-integration-test-*")
    17→	if err != nil {
    18→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to create temp dir for binary: %v\n", err)
    19→		os.Exit(1)
    20→	}
    21→
    22→	testBinaryPath = filepath.Join(tmpDir, "entire")
    23→
    24→	moduleRoot := findModuleRoot()
    25→	buildCmd := exec.Command("go", "build", "-o", testBinaryPath, ".")
    26→	buildCmd.Dir = filepath.Join(moduleRoot, "cmd", "entire")
    27→
    28→	buildOutput, err := buildCmd.CombinedOutput()
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to build CLI binary: %v\nOutput: %s\n", err, buildOutput)
    31→		os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
    32→		os.Exit(1)
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	// Run tests
    36→	code := m.Run()
    37→
    38→	// Cleanup
    39→	os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
    40→	os.Exit(code)
    41→}
